Industrial injuries disablement benefit Payments for care home, Abbeyfield Home or independent hospital charges Types of payment Payments for children and people living temporarily with the claimant 3. 2.	 10.	 Third party payments for care homes, Abbeyfield Homes or independent hospitals and National Healthmaintenance Note: the Independent Living Fund (2006) closed on 30.6.15 with some funding responsibilities transferring to LA’s in England, the Welsh Independent Living Grant in Wales and the Independent Living Fund Scotland for Northern Ireland and Scotland. Please contact DMA Leeds for advice if a clamant receives funding from any of these replacement schemes. Capital disregarded for 52 weeks? Arrears of allowances and benefits ?The law? The disregard also applies to a concessionary payment which is made to compensate for arrears of those benefits or allowances. The disregard applies from the date the concessionary payment is receivedThis list is not exhaustive, and the DM should consider all the reasons given by the claimant for the failure to undertake WRA. Georgia has agreed an action plan to engage in work-related activities. She has been made aware and fully understands that her benefit might be affected if she fails to undertake work-related activity when required to do so. to undertake work-related activity on this occasion due to her mental health state on the day. It was reasonable in her circumstances and she contacted the provider to let them know she could not attend. 4. Period of the reduction Length of fixed period Example 2 On 21.3.13 Julie fails without good cause to attend an interview with the provider where she was required to produce her CV, and the DM imposes a two week fixed period reduction after Julie agrees on 26.3.13 to attend a further appointment. See DMG 53091 and Chapter 04 for guidance on supersession where a reduction ends. "

It was reasonable in her circumstances and she contacted the provider to let them know she could not attend. 4. Period of the reduction Length of fixed period Example 2 On 21.3.13 Julie fails without good cause to attend an interview with the provider where she was required to produce her CV, and the DM imposes a two week fixed period reduction after Julie agrees on 26.3.13 to attend a further appointment. See DMG 53091 and Chapter 04 for guidance on supersession where a reduction ends. "

    "Note 1: The DM should not just consider one factor but should consider the overall picture of the claimant’s individual circumstances. The consideration is whether the reasons given for the specific failure to undertake work-related activity contributed to the claimant not complying with what we are expecting them to do and whether that was reasonable in the circumstances. DMs should also note the guidance on good cause in DMG Chapter 42. "
